# STATE

One line per session, newest last — what landed, and the measured number that
says it landed. The full §0.5 report for each session is in that session's
transcript; this file is the index a later session reads first.

## Live owed

LIVE steps built offline whose run waits on a need this machine lacked, one line each: `- §n.m — needs <what> — <the command to run>` (OF-BLD-014 A.4).

- §4.5, the paid lanes — needs a helper model the safety classifier lets through (Haiku is declined, bio) — `pnpm rag:eval && pnpm data:push`

- **2026-09-15 · OF-BLD-012.1 §6.1 (F1.1–F1.3)** — `export-corpus.ts` emits
  `range` and `negativeResult` as data instead of folding them into the
  `conditions` prose; §2.4 rule 3 reads the quote as quantities (units the
  paper wrote, the range it states, numbers written in words) instead of bare
  numbers, and records how it found the value as `valueBasis`; `biorepo.write`
  passes the record's recorded structure into anchoring. **30 of the 34**
  refused gold records now anchor, not the 32 the spec predicted: `r-A2-1` and
  `r-O8-1` refuse on rule 5, the field's own range, which no rule-3 fix
  reaches. `pnpm verify` green, all sixteen stages.
- **2026-09-15 · OF-BLD-012.1 §6.2 (F1.4–F1.5)** — `emit_candidates` gains
  `range` and `negativeResult` and the prompt says what each is for, so the
  extractor can say what the curators recorded; `match_run` scores a range
  candidate against a range record on its endpoints. `biorepo.write` grows a
  `dry_run`, `POST /api/biorepo/check` asks it, and Guild gates Accept and
  Gold on that answer with `writeRefusal` demoted to the offline fallback —
  the rules are not mirrored. New verify stage `check:anchors` holds the
  anchoring floor at **93 of 104**; seventeen stages now. Both new guards
  negative-tested. `pnpm verify` green.
- **2026-09-16 · OF-BLD-012.1 §6.3 (F2)** — decisions are signed by a person.
  Settings gains a Reviewer name field, kept in the Durable tier under
  `reviewerName`; `reviewerName()` in the store is the one place it is read and
  replaces all **12** hard-coded `'you'` sites (the spec said 10); an audit line
  made before a name is set says `this browser`, which can never be posted.
  `writeRefusal` gains a blocking `reviewer` rule while the service is up, and
  `biorepo.write` refuses a name under two characters or in the closed
  placeholder list (`you`, `me`, `reviewer`, `user`, `test`). The smoke now
  reviews as a named person and checks the posted decision carries that name;
  the durable check reloads the page and finds the name still there. Both new
  assertions negative-tested. `pnpm verify` green.
- **2026-09-16 · OF-BLD-012.1 §6.4 (F3)** — the overlay is authoritative.
  `Overlay.candidates` and `runs` are a list or `null` in both languages; a
  list replaces whatever its length, and only `null` keeps what was there. An
  undecided `haiku-1` record absent from an incoming list is removed from the
  store and the review queue; a decided one stays and carries `absentFromRun`,
  which the review card explains. New verify stage `check:overlay` drives the
  real store through five scenarios; eighteen stages now. Negative-tested.
  Correction to the spec: the eight store scenarios it says live in `scripts/`
  were scratchpad probes — this stage is the first one in the tree.
  `pnpm verify` green.
- **2026-09-16 · OF-BLD-012.1 §6.5 (F6–F7)** — `match_run` pairs by distance:
  every agreeing (record, candidate) pair is scored, sorted, and consumed
  greedily, ties breaking on record then candidate order, so the result no
  longer depends on arrival order and a record cannot take the candidate its
  sibling was closer to. The dropped-span pass is paired the same way and the
  docstring states the algorithm. `ExtractionRecord.original` is stamped once
  as a record enters the store and never touched by a decision;
  `reanchoredSpan` and the withdrawal of a correction read it instead of
  diffing against `RECORDS`, which a corpus update would move underneath a
  decided record. The overlay guard grew the F7 scenarios and is renamed
  `pnpm check:store`. Both fixes negative-tested. `pnpm verify` green.
- **2026-09-17 · OF-BLD-012.1 §6.6 (F8)** — a truncated paper is split, never
  discarded. `MAX_TOKENS` is 16 000 and no longer derived from Postdoc's; a
  response that hits the limit halves the paper by character count and asks
  again, twice deep, and only the remainder that still will not fit is
  reported. `ExtractResponse` gains `calls` and `truncatedSections` (the spec
  said `ExtractRun`; that model is Witness's per-run aggregate, and these are
  per-extraction facts that belong beside `usage`). Every call is paid for in
  `usage`. A paper that truncates all the way down is still refused and still
  cached as nothing. Negative-tested. `pnpm verify` green.
- **2026-09-17 · OF-BLD-012.1 §6.7 (F4–F5)** — the merge is tested and the
  guard brings its own export. `export-corpus.ts` takes
  `OPENFERMENT_CORPUS_OUT`; `check:biorepo` takes `OPENFERMENT_DATA_DIR` and
  exports into a temp directory itself, so it passes on a fresh clone with no
  `corpus.json`. New stage `pnpm test:export` exports the demo's decisions and
  checks the corrected value, the SI twin, the re-anchored quote, the appended
  record, gold provenance and the excluded rejection. The demo fixture now
  carries one of each kind of decision. **Tension resolved:** F4 asks the
  guard to fail on a bent value, but after F5 the guard builds the corpus it
  checks, so bending the decision bends both sides — rule 4 is testable
  through `OPENFERMENT_CORPUS_IN`, which nothing in verify sets. Nineteen
  stages. Both negative-tested. `pnpm verify` green.
- **2026-09-19 · OF-BLD-012.1 §6.8 (F9)** — identifiers proposed by machine,
  approved by a person. `pnpm ids:propose` asks Crossref about each of the
  **73** seed papers carrying no PMCID, DOI or PMID and writes a gitignored
  TSV; `pnpm ids:apply` writes back the rows a person changed to `APPROVED`
  and only those — under the entry's `venue:` line, never onto a paper that
  already has one, one line and nothing else — then prints the diff to commit,
  and raises rather than guess at an id the seed does not hold. Crossref lives
  in one function, so the matching, the verdict and the patcher are pinned
  offline: **19** tests in verify, plus one `live` test that is the only place
  an unreachable Crossref shows up as a refusal instead of 73 silent REVIEW
  rows. Guild gains the **Papers the service can keep** filter over the **52**
  unverified records `biorepo.write` refuses for want of an identifier.
  **Two findings.** The filter as first written was decoration: Guild seeded
  its queue at mount, before `/api/health` had answered, so the box read
  "hiding 52" above a queue still holding all 135 — the smoke's old `/ 135`
  assertion passing is what proved it. The queue waits for the answer now,
  which cost the smoke its cold-open race for §7.3's "an arriving candidate
  joins an open queue"; that contract moved into `check:store`, where it needs
  no clock. And **Crossref is unreachable from this environment** — the agent
  proxy answers 403 to CONNECT for `api.crossref.org` — so `ids:propose` is
  Sean's to run; from here every lookup degrades to a REVIEW row, as designed.
  Four guards negative-tested. `pnpm verify` green, nineteen stages.
- **2026-09-21 · OF-BLD-012.1 §6.9 (review pass, truth pass, PR)** — the
  thirty commits since `main` were read adversarially before the PR, with the
  store and the service driven by probes. **Fifteen findings, all confirmed,
  all fixed, each with a test that failed first** (`a264368`). The ones that
  mattered: a live fetch applied its result with `candidates: []`, which under
  F3 wiped the extractor's candidates on every fetch; a reviewer-typed
  correction with no quote skipped rule 3 and reached `corpus.json` inside no

  read "one of the highest titres" as a titre of 1 and matched negation

  ten; `applyOverlay` moved the reviewer to a different card when the queue
  was pruned; the deep-link effect re-fired on every queue change and, opened
  early, defeated F9's filter. `check:anchors` still **93 of 104**; **318**
  Python tests. Truth pass over README: three stages missing from the gate
  (`test:export`, `check:anchors`, `check:store`), **15** strains not 7, the
  F9 commands and the reviewer name in "Running it", dates re-measured (still
  0 fetched, 0 extracted, 0 verified in this checkout). `CLAUDE.md` already
  carried F1's `valueBasis` list and the `/check` rule from §6.1–6.2; it
  gains the typed-value and withdrawal rules. `pnpm verify` green, nineteen
  stages. PR opened to `main`.
- **2026-09-22 · OF-BLD-012.1 §6.9 (security pass)** — the branch read once
  more, for what an attacker rather than a reviewer would find. **One
  vulnerability, confirmed and closed:** every script that serves `dist/` —
  the smoke, the five browser checks and `pnpm demo:offline` — resolved the
  request path with `join(DIST, url)`, which collapses `..`, so
  `/../core/.env` read the key off the disk of whoever was running the demo,
  and the demo listened on every interface while it did. The seven copies are
  one confined handler now, `scripts/lib/serve-dist.mjs`; a request that
  leaves `dist/` is a 404, every server binds to loopback, and the demo takes
  `OPENFERMENT_DEMO_HOST` to be shown to another machine on purpose. The
  smoke sends four traversal shapes raw and expects four 404s; with the
  confinement removed it says so. **Two hardening fixes, below the bar as
  vulnerabilities and taken anyway:** a DOI is written into a TypeScript
  string literal the gate then executes, so `ids:apply` refuses — never
  repairs — any value that is not `10.NNNN/suffix` free of quotes and
  whitespace, and `ids:propose` marks such a hit REVIEW; and a candidate id's
  paper segment is `[A-Za-z0-9_]+`, so `_resolve` cannot be asked to open
  `candidates/../../x.json`. Examined and clean: the API's `paper_id` is
  checked against the corpus before it reaches a path; JATS parsing resolves
  no external entities; no `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`; the workflow uses
  `pull_request`, not `pull_request_target`. `pnpm verify` green, nineteen
  stages.
- **2026-10-09 · OF-BLD-013 Phase 1 (§1.1–§1.4)** — Guild's competence

  withdrawals in both languages (five new `PAIRS`), written only by

  `POST /api/guild/check` as the dry run and `offlineRefusal` as the labelled
  fallback; `core/data/guild.json` is gitignored. Levels are computed by
  `competenceOf` and never stored. Guild gains Matrix, People and Skills
  beside Review, a sign-off sheet, ledger export and an invented, labelled
  sample team that is never posted or kept. New stage `check:guild`; twenty
  stages now. Every rule negative-tested. `pnpm verify` green at each commit.

- **2026-10-09 · OF-BLD-013 Phase 3 (§3.1–§3.3)** — path and knowledge.
  A lesson may name skills; passing its checkpoint writes knowledge with no
  observer for whoever is learning, which makes **Learning and no more**:
  Supervised still needs an assessor's training sign-off (the plan's
  "knowledge evidence complete", read as the sign-off, because a checkpoint
  can be answered by anyone holding the tablet). A placeholder bench track of

## OF-BLD-015 Phase 7 and OF-BLD-017 Phase 5 (branch `claude/of-bld-015-postdoc`, from `main` at `6097df7`)

- **Model rule, 2026-10-19** — every model call openFerment makes runs `claude-haiku-5-5` at effort `medium`: primary, fallback, index, eval (`OPENFERMENT_MODEL`, `OPENFERMENT_FALLBACK_MODEL`, `OPENFERMENT_INDEX_MODEL`, `OPENFERMENT_EFFORT` on the environment; `llm.py` defaults untouched). `pnpm ready` on 2026-10-19: lane `full`, answer and index roles both `claude-haiku-5-5`, $0.00 spent.
- **Part A, measured 2026-10-19 on `6097df7`** — before any change:
  - `core/openferment_core/tools/__init__.py` 164 lines. `WHITELIST` = `("search_records", "convert_units", "search_passages", "yield_ceiling", "growth_forecast", "selling_price", "draft_runbook")`; `COUNT_FIELDS` = `{"k", "limit"}`; `MAX_SUMMARY_CHARS` = 9,000. Tool files: `fermos.py` 56, `geneos.py` 70, `passages.py` 77, `proforma.py` 72, `records.py` 63, `runbook.py` 76, `units.py` 129 lines. None reads Dominion, the protocols, the strains, the runbooks, the ledger, the depositions, the artifacts as a list, or the Guild: the prompt's statement holds.
  - `postdoc_agent.py` 353 lines: `_plan_schema`, `Cancelled`, `_Run` (with `step`), `_tool_defs`, `_count`, `_summary_line`, `_run_tool`, `_out_of_budget`, `_attempt`, `ask`.
  - `api.py` 872 lines: `POST /api/ask` (line 153, `agent` mode through `_ask_agent`, `single` through `postdoc.py`), `POST /api/ask/stream` (line 225, `step` events then one `plan` or `error`). `models.py` 1,448 lines; `AskRequest` carries `question`, `maxEvidence` (30) and `mode` (`agent` | `single`), as the prompt says.
  - `src/store.ts` 3,646 lines; the chat section (lines 2254–2290) keeps sessions in memory alone: `createSession` (new id, prepended), `appendMessage`, `updateMessage`, `pinEvidence` (deduplicated by paper and section). `src/data/misc.ts` 126 lines; `SEED_SESSIONS` holds two sessions with no messages (`sess-seed-1` "Has anyone expressed a casein in an alga?", `sess-seed-2` "Reading H4 on P. pastoris β-casein", scoped to H4); `check:seed` walks them.
  - `mcp_server.py` 89 lines: lists `tools.infos()` and calls `tools.run`, as the prompt says.
  - Where the module data lives: Dominion's molecules and findings, the runbooks, protocols, strains, pathways, unit operations, scenarios and lessons are TypeScript seed the service never saw (`corpus.json` carried product and strain ids alone); records, candidates, artifacts, Guild's ledger and checks, Intake's fetch state and the geneOS shelf are the service's.
  - Two facts differ from what the tree says of itself: the seed holds **119** molecules where `Dominion.tsx`'s header says 117; and `docs/build/` (OF-BLD-014's document and prompt) is still on `main`, although OF-BLD-014 §10.4 says it is deleted before the pull request. Neither blocks this build.
  - `pnpm verify` before any change: every stage through `test:reconcile` green; `test:smoke` then failed on the new trace line, because this session's §7.1 fixtures were regenerated while the run was going. The tree at `6097df7` is the one STATE's fix pass measured 23 of 23.
- **§7.1, the coverage pass — 2026-10-19** — `pnpm export:corpus` writes a third projection, `modules.json` (gitignored), with the seed's modules: **119** molecules and **2** clearance findings, **10** runbooks and **9** protocols, **17** hosts and **11** pathways, **27** unit operations, **4** scenarios, **12** lessons. `module_stores.py` reads all eleven modules into one row shape and computes the one `state` enum from each module's own word (a clearance of `clear-expired` or `clear-none` is `open`; `watch-*`, `blocked` and `unknown` are `fenced`, because Dominion's action for `unknown` is to run clearance before any commitment; a locked runbook is `locked`). `coverage(question)` is code, no model: rows named in the question by label, id or spelling, and every row of a module the question names, kept to the states a clause about that module asks for. Before round one `postdoc_agent.ask` sweeps, puts the map in round one after the question, adds a `coverage` trace step that opens to one line a module, and the plan carries `coverage`; the footer says "Searched eleven, found in …" with the modules read only in part (Runbooks, fermOS, BioRepo, Home). A plan with no claim never says the corpus is silent when a module found something. "What is open in Dominion and which runbooks touch it?" on this checkout: Dominion **56** open, Runbooks **19** (the question names them), searched eleven, found in two. `test_coverage.py` (11 tests) names a Dominion molecule, a runbook and a strain against the seeded projection. `pnpm verify` green, 23 of 23; **1,285** Python tests offline.
- **§7.2, a read tool per module — 2026-10-19** — eleven read tools join the registry (eighteen names in `WHITELIST`): `dominion_read`, `runbooks_read`, `geneos_read`, `fermos_read`, `pureos_read`, `proforma_read`, `biorepo_read`, `guild_read`, `primer_read`, `intake_read`, `home_read`. **The names carry an underscore where the build document writes a dot**: the Messages API refused `dominion.read` with `tools.0.custom.name: String should match pattern '^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{1,128}$'` and accepted `dominion_read` (the free token-count endpoint, 2026-10-19, $0). One input for all eleven (ids or refs, words, a kind, a state, a limit; a number anywhere but `limit` is refused as before); one output, `ModuleRow` (ref `<module>/<kind>/<id>`, label, the computed `state`, the module's own status word, the provenance mark or null, a short detail), at most 20 rows and inside `MAX_SUMMARY_CHARS`, with the count of every match by state and the module's `partial` note. Guild reads a name, a role and whether the person is active, and the skills a check names; no title, no evidence, no level. BioRepo reads records, candidates, artifacts and Witness runs. `SeenSet` keeps the rows a result returned; a claim cites them in `moduleRefs`, and `validate_plan` drops a claim citing a ref no tool returned; a claim's provenance takes the rows' marks into its weakest, `unverified` on unmarked rows alone. The plan carries the cited rows and the answer shows each as a link with its state. On this checkout the modules hold 121 Dominion rows (56 open), 19 Runbooks, 41 geneOS, 0 fermOS, 27 pureOS, 5 Proforma, 492 BioRepo, 0 Guild, 12 Primer, 167 Intake, 337 Home. `test_module_tools.py` (19 tests) ends with "What is open in Dominion?" answered by a stand-in from `dominion_read`, its claim citing the row and the footer naming the modules. `pnpm verify` green, 23 of 23; **1,304** Python tests offline.
- **§7.2, fixed after its first live run — 2026-10-19** — the committed §7.2 failed every live question. First the API refused the request: `Schemas contains too many parameters with union types (25 …; limit: 16)`, because each read tool carried a nullable `kind` and `state`; they are plain fields now, an empty string meaning any, and `test_module_tools` counts the unions a request sends (negative-tested). Then: `The compiled grammar is too large … reduce the number of strict tools.` Probed with `tool_choice` `auto` on Haiku: the seven earlier tools pass strict; eighteen strict fail even with the read schemas cut to ids, words and limit; eighteen pass with the eleven reads loose. So a `Tool` carries `strict` (default true), `llm.tool_param` sends it, and the read tools go out loose; `tools.run` validates their input with the same Pydantic model, extra fields and numbers refused, a refusal returned in words. **This departs from CLAUDE.md's "Postdoc runs strict tools" for the reads alone, and is Sean's to accept.** The probes cost under $0.01.
- **§7.2, live — 2026-10-19** — "What is open in Dominion and which runbooks touch it?" through `postdoc_agent.ask` on `claude-haiku-5-5`, effort medium: coverage (searched eleven, found in two), then `dominion_read` (molecules, open: 55), `runbooks_read` (runbooks: 10), `dominion_read` by four refs, `dominion_read` "polymerase"; **4 claims kept**, each citing module rows (Pfu, T7 and T4 polymerases open; thermostable DNA ligase open, touched by `rb-thermo-ligase`; malaria antigens open, touched by `rb-diag-panel`; `rb-lyo-ambient` names no product), **2 dropped** for typing counts, 2 gaps dropped for the same; 3 gaps kept, one naming what the browser holds. **$0.0047, 17.7 s.**
- **§7.2, tuned on live runs — 2026-10-20** — eight cross-module questions through the built app (`pnpm prototype:up`, the stream route, `claude-haiku-5-5` at medium), twice: **$0.0358** then **$0.0345**, 8 to 34 s each. Every question read two to four modules; Haiku called the read tools by ref, by words and by state, and `yield_ceiling`, `selling_price` and `search_passages` where they fit. What the first run exposed, and what changed: claims dropped for a typed count or ordinal, four → **one** after prompt rules 10 and 11 (no numerals, counts or ordinals; draft when asked); an artifact row said only its tool and date, so a row now carries the artifact's top-level scalars and its inputs' names (the Proforma benchmark reads `mespUsdPerGal`); a word search was whole-token ("depos" found nothing), so a query word of four letters or more now matches the head of a row's word, and a lesson's words include its module's blurb; a molecule row carries its `processCode`, `unitOperationIds` and `pathwayIds`, the seams to pureOS and geneOS. **A runbook was drafted live** for the first time on Haiku: "Draft a runbook for PHB in C. necator H16 on fructose …" ran the ceiling, had its first `draft_runbook` refused for a measure's unit, corrected it, and returned a draft with the ceiling as a bound; 7 claims kept, 0 dropped, $0.0058, 23 s. Asked for xylose instead it searched, found no H16 record on xylose, and drafted nothing, which is right. The screen was driven with Playwright against the real service: the sweep opens to eleven lines, the read lines open to their inputs, the claims carry row tags with states, the footer reads "Searched eleven, found in two: Dominion (1), Runbooks (1) · read in part: Runbooks, fermOS, BioRepo, Home"; `docs/shots/of-bld-015-postdoc-brazzein.png`. The run sheet for showing it is `docs/build/DEMO-PHASE7.md`. Three tests pin the changes. `pnpm verify` green, 23 of 23; **1,309** Python tests offline. Day's ledger: **$0.099** over the runs above.
- **§7.3, a write tool per module — 2026-10-20** — eleven write tools join the registry (29 names): one input (`action`, `target`, `fields` of names to words, `note`), and a write tool never touches a store: each action calls the module's own write function (`geneos.ceiling`/`frontier`/`mix`, `fermos.forecast_run`, `proforma.run`, `extract.add_candidate` under §2.4's anchoring with ids `mw1-…`, `intake.fetch_paper`, `extract.extract_paper`) or refuses in words and tells Postdoc to say so in `gaps`: Dominion, pureOS and Primer are curated seed with no write function; a protocol revision, a deposition's fit, a Proforma assumption, a review decision and a new source each name the person who makes them. Every write that goes through lands in the open lane and is appended to `core/data/audit.jsonl` (gitignored, `GET /api/audit`) with `agent: model`, the model id, the effort and the ask id (`ToolContext.ask_id`, set per ask); a candidate the model wrote by hand carries an `AuditEvent` naming the model beside the extractor's. **Measured on this checkout with a scratch data directory:** `dominion_write` refuses; `runbooks_write lock`, `guild_write propose_check` and `home_write note` come back `proposed` and write nothing but the proposal; `geneos_write ceiling` keeps artifact `207a9865f4a8c578` as computed; `intake_write fetch P1` prepares 64 sections; `biorepo_write candidate` refuses on rule `value` when the number is not in the sentence; `proforma_write run` refuses naming the four inputs still waiting. `test_writes.py` (33 tests). `pnpm verify` green, 23 of 23; **1,342** Python tests offline.
- **§7.4, the gates — 2026-10-20** — one table, `core/openferment_core/tools/gates.py` and the same table in `docs/TOOLS.md`: `publish`, `spend`, `lock`, `sign`, `personnel`, `bench`, `notebook`, each with the (module, action) pairs it stops and what the one button does. A gated action writes nothing: `proposals.propose` keeps it in `core/data/proposals.json` (gitignored) with `state: proposed`, the model's id, the ask and the note; `GET /api/proposals`, `POST /api/proposals/{id}/carry-out` and `/dismiss` take a named person (placeholders refused, as `biorepo.write` refuses them). The single-call path filters its records too. The MCP server takes the switch from `OPENFERMENT_INCLUDE_ARCHIVED` (unset: off), never from a client. Browser: `src/lib/postdoc.ts` sends the store's `showArchived` with every question, and `src/lib/rag.ts` (the real place `/api/rag/search` is called; not named by the document) with every search. **Pool on this checkout, switch off: 163** (thread Q's candidates alone; 168 casein and 303 PHA hidden); **switch on: 634**, exactly the old algorithm's count (134 seed records, 500 candidates). The anaerobic reaction is two in parallel (Glucose → 2 Ethanol + 2 CO2 for the ethanol made; Glucose → Yeast + CO2 + H2O, atom-balanced with no oxygen, for the cells made), and glucose the batch used beyond them stays in the broth, flagged, as `ethanol_glucose_v0` leaves it. The vessel type is a request field (`vesselType`, also on `ParamResolveRequest`), a `convention` input in the basis like a run's flowsheet, not an override; a bubble column's `kW_per_m3` is a convention 0 (else BioSTEAM costs an agitator). `batch` is not offered (continuous mode divides by one hour where the batch time belongs: kLa off by τ), nor `adiabatic`, `tau_phases`, `reactions`, `kLa_kwargs`, `Nmin`, `Nmax`; the scope's notes say each. `batch_volume` is one vessel's working volume and needs `loading_time`; it is not offered for the NREL vessel, which refuses one vessel. A plant scale reads the corn TEA's operating hours and the titre at harvest; a simulation with no product refuses a plant scale in words. Both scales, or N and V_max both overridden, are refused. A value whose source is the class's own default is left to the class (not sent to the worker); everything else is sent. "Until the sugar runs out" doubles its horizon from 1/μmax (at most 40 times) and refuses a run whose sugar stops falling; the spike's 10,000 h cap is gone. (5) D5's exponential feed underfeeds (0.079 against μset 0.15); the feed pays the whole demand and holds 0.151. (6) D5's washout expression is optimistic with kd or Ki; both it and μ(Sf) − kd are reported. (7) The feed's start and end split the integration. (8) storage_v1: Yps is the store's yield, α, β, Pmax and n are not its parameters, Q starts at zero, and Q/Qmax reads Q/(X + Q). (9) The mass balance (consumption states, and the cross-check through the yields) catches a wrong rate law (a dropped 1/Yps reads 30 % off) but never integrator error; the integration is tested against an rtol 1e-12 reference. The doc's carbon balance needs carbon fractions that are not parameters (biomass has no formula), so it is not built. (10) OUR's peak is read on the solver's steps. (11) The logistic fit's final curve starts a Monod fit far off (X0 0.017 against 0.2, μ 0.93 against 0.40 on the stand-in, and the fit then failed); a family fit starts from the logistic fit's own first estimates read off the readings (the growth window's μ, Luedeking–Piret and yields by bounded linear least squares, Ks at half S0), and fits X0, S0 and P0 too (D6's first readings biased a noisy fit: μ 0.540 against 0.40). (12) A product-and-substrate-only fit pins μmax by its numbers; §3.1's "μ unidentified" is a rule, with the numbers kept in `identified_by_numbers` and said in words. With no biomass series X0 is held at a gauge of 1 g L⁻¹, and Yx/s, α and β, which scale with it, are reported unidentified and freed from their spec bounds. (13) `pinv` hides rank deficiency: a parameter in the Jacobian's null space (SVD, `RANK_TOL`) is unidentified and its finite interval is said to understate it (an infinite interval cannot be stored: the artifact store refuses non-finite JSON). (14) Appendix 2 §A names fields the ontology lacks (maintenance_coefficient, biomass_yield_oxygen); their rules read `fit, assumption, convention` until §3.2 adds them. `pha_productivity` is per litre, so `q_storage_max` binds no record directly, and `pha_content_cdw` is in % CDW, so `q_max` takes the share limit 0 to 1. (15) scipy was already imported in `flowsheets/ethanol_glucose_v0.py` (worker side) and in `demo.py` (the fixture builder, which no route imports); test_workers allows both, with the reason. **Choices left open, and what I chose:** the kinetic fit's tool version is bumped to `fermos-2`, because its output now holds `model` and `notes`. A fit kept under `fermos-1` reads as logistic_v0, and the nightly replay reports it stale, not different; a new fit of the same deposition is a new artifact. Appendix 2 §A's n range (0.5 to 3) is not typed: kinetics.py holds no number but its solver's settings, so n's bound is the physical one (above zero) and its reason says so. Named records bind ahead of a fit or a kept simulation (a person's override still wins); the basis names each as a `<key> record` input and adds `<key> origin` = `records named` (`fermos.NAMED`, note `NAMED_NOTE`); `records of` is added only when the rule bound some record; a request naming none keeps the basis, and so the artifact id, it had before. `fermos_read` gains kind `simulation` (`module_stores._simulations`): ref `fermos/simulation/<artifact id>`, label `<title> (<family>) simulation of <strain label | every strain of the program's organism>`, `open`/`computed`, the mark from `_artifact_mark` (recomputed on read), a detail of words, ids and booleans only (family, title, strain, product, sugar, `boundBy` {origin: [keys]}, aerobic, aboveCeiling, flagged, cite). `validate.py`: `KEPT_RUNS` {`fermos.simulation`: (fermos, simulation)} and `SeenSet.runs` {artifact id: row ref}, learned from an Evidence whose basis tool is a kept run and from a `fermos/simulation/<id>` row; a claim citing by evidence id a simulation a read returned only as a row is dropped with words naming the ref to cite.
